URBAN DEVELOPMENT DEPARTMENT
Mantralaya, Mumbai - 400 032
Dated 25th March 2025
The Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966
Corrigendum
No. TPS-1024/02/CR-42/EP Publish/Corrigendum/2024/UD-9.– Whereas, the Development Plan
of Sakri Nagarpanchayat, district Dhule has been partly sanctioned by the Government vide Urban
Development Department's Notiﬁ cation No. TPS-1024/02/CR-42/DP Part Sanction/2024/UD-9, dated
the 14th January 2025 and the Republished Development Plan of Excluded Parts of substantial natures
modiﬁ cations, has been published by the Government vide Urban Development Department's Notice
No.TPS-1024/02/CR-42/EP Publish/2024/UD-9, dated 14th January 2025 (hereinafter referred to as
"the said Notice") under Section 31 (1) of the Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning
Act, 1966;
and whereas, some corrections are required to be made in "Schedule-B'' appended to the said
Notice.
Now, therefore, this Corrigendum is being issued to amend "Schedule-B" appended to the said
Notice, as mentioned in the Table below and the said Notice in being amended accordingly–

BY CHIEF OFFICER
RAHATA MUNICIPAL COUNCIL
Publication of Draft Development Plan (Revised) of Rahata Under Section 26 (1) of
Maharashtra Regional Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966
Notice
No. Town Planning/66/2025.– Whereas, the Rahata Municipal Council, (hereinafter referred to
as “the said Planning Authority”) vide Resolution No. 24, dated 7th January 2022, had declared it's
intention to prepare a Draft Development Plan (Revised) of Rahata [hereinafter referred to as the
“said Draft Development Plan (Revised)”] for the area under it's jurisdiction, (hereinafter referred to
as the “said area”) as per the provision of Section 23 (1) read with 38 of Maharashtra Regional
Planning and Town Planning Act, 1966 (hereinafter referred to as the “said Act”) and notice to that
eﬀ ect is published in Maharashtra Government Gazette, Nashik Divisional, Supplement, Part 1 on
and whereas, as per the provision in Section 24 of the said Act, the Municipal Council vide Order
No. 83, dated 1st June 2022 has appointed Assistant Director, Town Planning, Ahilyanagar Branch,
Ahilyanagar as “Town Planning Oﬃ cer” to prepare the said Draft Development Plan (Revised) for the
said area. Also, as per the provision in Section 24 of the said Act, the Municipal Council vide Order
No. 171, dated 13th September 2024 has appointed Assistant Director, Town Planning, Shrirampur
Branch, Shrirampur as “Town Planning Oﬃ cer” to prepare the said Draft Development Plan (Revised)
for the said area;
and whereas, the Town Planning Oﬃ cer and Assistant Director, Town Planning, Ahilyanagar
Branch, Ahilyanagar after having prepared Existing Land Use Map as per Section 25 of the said Act,
Town Planning Oﬃ cer and Assistant Director, Town Planning, Shrirampur Branch, Shrirampur has
prepared the said Draft Development Plan (Revised) of the said area and has handed it over to the
said Planning Authority on 1st April 2025 for publication under Section 26 (1) of the said Act;
and whereas, the said Planning Authority has passed Resolution No. 1, dated 1st April 2025 to
publish the said Draft Development Plan (Revised) under Section 26 (1) of the said Act, for inviting
suggestions / objections from the public.
Therefore, Rahata Municipal Council hereby publishes the said Draft Development Plan (Revised)
along with the Report under Section 26 (1) of the said Act for inviting suggestions / objections from
the public. The copy of the said Draft Development Plan (Revised) and Report thereof are kept open
for inspection of the public at the following oﬃ ces on all working days during oﬃ ce hours.
1. The Chief Oﬃ cer, Rahata Municipal Council.
2. Assistant Director, Town Planning, Shrirampur Branch, Shrirampur
The suggestions / objections if any, in writing from the public in this regard with reasons, shall be
submitted to the Chief Oﬃ cer, Rahata Municipal Council, within the period of 30 days from the date
of publication of the Notice in Maharashtra Government Gazette. Suggestions / objections received by
the Municipal Council within the period of the 30 days from the date of publication of this Notice in
Maharashtra Government Gazette shall only be considered.
The copies of the said Draft Development Plan (Revised) and particulars shall be available to
the public at reasonable price in the oﬃ ce of the Rahata Municipal Council.
VAIBHAV LONDHE,
Chief Oﬃ cer,
Rahata Municipal Council, Rahata,
District Ahilyanagar.
Rahata, 2nd April 2025.
